# X·F CENSUS-CANONICAL — the one census of record

**THE CENSUS OF RECORD — supersedes every prior denominator, g19 operand, and per-spec census claim; a gate that cites another census operand is DEFECTIVE on sight.** *(Stamped by the BLESS seat, round 4, 2026-08-29, after the hostile re-derivation recorded at §0.3.)*

**Seat**: census-canonical seat, X·F. **Written**: 2026-08-29. **Operand**: the 66 frozen
`fr-*.md` records at `docs/tranches/V/megatranche/registry/adjudicated/`, read whole, one at a
time. **Twin partition operand**: `waves/F-W3.md` §X.1-v4 (the definitive file-criterion
partition, repair round 3, ruling R3-1a). **Defect classes consumed**: `PASS-4/UNION.md` §1c
(the atomiser that fabricates — P4-1's 30-id prefix-axis tier) and §2 (fabrication), plus the
alias law F-W4 proved.

**WHY THIS FILE EXISTS.** Four conformance passes oscillated because every round re-invented the
detector: pass 1 anchored on table shape, pass 2 on row shape, pass 3 on record/position, pass 4
on scope — and the ruled atomiser of round 3 (`split on / ≡ ⊕ ∘ , +`) began MANUFACTURING
bookings by atomising `D/D-18` onto a foreign record's `D-18`. This file ends that by fixing the
derivation ONCE. **It is the SOLE census operand for every gate and every future pass.** A gate
that disagrees with this file re-derives against it or amends it by ruling — it does not re-cut a
detector.

---

## §0 DERIVATION RULES — stated verbatim, then applied uniformly to all 66 records

1. **A ROW is a banked-head line.** The **LEFT token of a bold head is the banked id**; every
   `·`/slash-joined trailing token is an **ALIAS carried beside it, never a standalone identity**.
2. **A row's ROUTING is the X-token(s) on its line, or its terminal disposition** — any phrasing,
   both dash spellings, table or bullet or prose.
3. **A row's HOME is**: sole-token → that wave; **F.W3/W4 dual → the twin law** (file-criterion
   per §X.1-v4: the record's target file appears in an F-W3 §5 unit file list `.a`–`.e` ⇒ home
   **F.W3**, else **F.W4**); **F.W7 → nothing routes there** (the ∅ posture, closed);
   **NO-WAVE-OWNER → the packet home**.
4. **NO position-restricted or count-based probes** — enumerate by reading each record whole.

### §0.1 The four rules made operational (the readings this seat applied, stated so no later seat re-derives them)

- **HEAD (rule 1).** A head is the row's identity span — the leading bold span of a list item or
  prose row, or the identity cell of a table row — **bolded or bare**. Bold is the common
  spelling, not the criterion: records book rows with unbolded identity cells
  (`| 2 | D:B-2 · C:C-1 | MAJOR | …`), and a bold-only probe is precisely the row-shape blindness
  pass 2 convicted. Every line of every record was examined; no line was excluded by position.
- **SEPARATOR SEMANTICS (rule 1, and the cure for P4-1's atomiser).** Inside a head:
  - **QUALIFIER** — `·` `:` `/` standing between a **bare axis token** (no dash of its own: `D`,
    `L`, `C`, `m`, `i`, `DU`) and an id-shaped remainder bind into **ONE id**: `D/D-18`, `L·S-1`,
    `C:C-3`, `D:B-1`. `/` is protected here **exactly as `·` already is** — this is the pass-4
    ruling that ends the manufactured foreign-record bookings.
  - **ALIAS** — `=` `/` `·` `⊕` `∘` `+` `,` standing between two **already id-shaped** tokens:
    the left one is the banked id, the rest are aliases (`D-1/L-1/C-B1` → banked `D-1`, aliases
    `L-1` `C-B1`; `N-10 · D-m9/D-m10` → banked `N-10`, the rest cross-reference axes).
  - **ROW-SEPARATOR** — ` · ` at the TOP level of a class-label list line
    (`**Minors (17 carried):** L-6 · D-19/D-20/D-31 · …`) separates **rows**, not aliases.
- **ONE HOME PER ID (rule 1's consequence).** A banked id is booked **once per record**: its
  banking line is its first banked-head line inside a register (roster · folds · killed-claims ·
  superlatives · UNPROVEN/residue · dissent · missed-finds · relay); later banked-head lines
  carrying the same left token are **citations, not second rows**. An id **claimed as an alias**
  by another row in the same record is **not a standalone identity** and books no row of its own.
- **ROUTING (rule 2).** Tokens are recorded **verbatim as spelled** (`F.W3/W4`, `F.W5-W8`,
  `F.W5–W8`, `F.W9/W10`, `NO-WAVE-OWNER`, `GLASS-RELAY`, `SS-13`, `LATEX-PAPER`, `KF.W7`). Where
  a banking line states no routing, the row's routing is taken from that id's other banked-head
  lines **in its own record** and the source is disclosed in the column (`record (:NNN)`) rather
  than trimmed to a false ∅.
- **HOME (rule 3), the multi-token cases the rule does not name.** Where a row carries more than
  one wave token, the **home is the first wave token its own arrow names** (the adopting wave) and
  every further token is a **LEG/RIDER** recorded beside it — the §X.1 idiom
  (`**F.W4** (primitive leg F.W1)`). Band spellings (`F.W5-W8`, `F.W9/W10`) are **sole tokens**
  and home at the band as spelled. Relay tokens (`GLASS-RELAY`, `SS-n`, `KF.W*`, `LATEX-PAPER`)
  are never a wave home; alone, they are the row's packet home. A row with neither a wave nor a
  packet token but with a terminal disposition homes at **TERMINAL (∅)**; a row with neither
  homes at **UNROUTED** and is counted as such — never silently dropped.
- **TERMINAL DISPOSITION BY REGISTER (rule 2's "any phrasing").** A row that states no X-token
  takes the disposition of the register it is banked in, because that register **is** the phrasing:
  a **killed-claims** entry is killed (whatever the verb — KILLED · SUSTAINED · RATIFIED ·
  OVERTURNED), a **superlative** is ratified, a **preserved dissent** is preserved, a
  **ruled-disagreement / seat-correction** head is ruled — all four are **TERMINAL (∅)**, holding
  no wave. An **UNPROVEN-NEEDS-LIVE / residue** item takes its register's own declared
  destination, **SS-13**. Nothing else is inferred: a roster or carried-roster row with no token
  anywhere in its record is **UNROUTED** and is counted as UNROUTED (307 rows — *corrected 2026-08-29; this parenthetical read 252 against its own §3 table* — overwhelmingly
  r1-carried rosters whose routings live in the superseded revision, and identity-fold rows whose
  route is the banked target's, not this record's). The UNROUTED bucket is a **finding**, not a
  gap in this file: it is the exact population a routing gate must resolve.
- **F.W7 (rule 3), verified rather than assumed.** ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, over all 66 records:
  `grep -o "F\.W7" fr-*.md` → **2 hits, both inside `KF.W7`** (the keyframes cross-repo relay at
  `fr-Tooltip`), and `grep -o "F\.W6\|F\.W8\|F\.W10"` → **∅**. **No row in any record routes
  F.W7** — the ∅ posture is the corpus's own reading, not a wave-side convention, and F.W6 · F.W8 ·
  F.W10 are likewise unnamed by any record (their wave-side populations are folds and operands,
  never record routings).
- **NOT A BANKING SITE.** The **preamble** (Seat · Date · Subject · Corpus · Method · instruments —
  everything above `## Scoreboard`) and the **Scoreboard** itself are summaries, never banking
  sites; heads occurring only there book no row.
- **BANDS.** A banded head (`FM-4..FM-16`, `K-1..K-16`) is ONE banked-head line naming a
  contiguous id range; the band is carried as one row and its members are enumerated in the
  per-wave roster so no member is lost to the band's shorthand.

### §0.3 HOSTILE RE-DERIVATION, ROUND 4 (2026-08-29) — ten records re-derived whole under §0's own rules; every divergence fixed IN PLACE below

The BLESS seat re-derived **fr-ContourEditorCanvas · fr-GalleryDraftsSection · fr-CoefficientsSpectrum · fr-DarkModeToggle · fr-EasingPicker · fr-CollapsibleSection · fr-EqCoefficientsPanel · fr-CoefficientsPanel · fr-EditorControlsDock · fr-VisualizationView** against their frozen records. Three divergence CLASSES were found and cured in place (each carries a dated note at its record):

1. **Axis-stripped / manufactured identities** (the census's own inverse of P4-1's atomiser): short-form citations booked as fresh rows — CEC's six UNPROVEN heads (`B-3`≡`D/B-3` …, proven by the record's own closing verdict), EP's six ruled/killed short forms (`D-9`≡`D/D-9` …), ECD's `MAJ-8`≡`D-10`, DMT's `level-3`, VV's `r1-carried` — 15 rows REMOVED; their SS-13/terminal content re-attached as legs on the banked rows. Conversely, **five rows the derivation DROPPED are readmitted**: CS `R-6`, EP `D/D-19`, EqCP `D/M-8`, VV `M1`/`A-1`/`D-14`/`MIN-8` (7 adds).
2. **Home inversions against §0.1's first-arrow rule**: rows homed at a rider wave their own arrow never names — GDS `M-2`/`F-6` (noted at the record, tables corrected round-4), CollS `m-7`/`m-14`/`i-7`, EqCP `R-4`/`FR-EQC-2`/`FR-EQC-5`/`FR-EQC-7`/`D/m-12`, CP `FR-CP-10`/`FR-CP-11`/`FR-CP-20`/`FR-CP-21`/`FR-CP-27`/`FR-CP-44`, ECD `D-1`/`L-2`/`L-6`/`C-10`/`L-5`/`L-14`, VV `C-2` — all re-homed to the arrow-named wave/packet, riders demoted to legs.
3. **Alias mangles/fabrications**: CEC `D/M-10` missing `L-18`; CS `K-9` `M-10`→`D:M-10`, `K-11` +`L:S4`, `K-3` +4; GDS `K-6` `D·D-M-1`→`D-M-1` (noted); DMT `M-5` −`M-6`; EP `L/i-5` +`S-4`, `D/X-D1` −`L/M-4`; EqCP `FR-EQC-2` `FR-EQC-10`→`missed-4`, `D/i-3` `fr-App`→`MG-β`; CP `FR-CP-2` −`FR-CP-11`, `FR-CP-9` `L·C-4`→`C-4`, `FR-CP-17` −`FR-CP-35`; ECD `D-5`/`L-7` cleared; VV `C-1` −`L-27`, `C-2` −`D-18`, `M2` −`VV-R2-D`.

**Net**: ROWS 4278 → **4269**; §2 rosters, §3 totals and §4 derivations re-based below. §0.1's UNROUTED parenthetical corrected (it read 252 against its own table). GDS's two manufactured rows (`Zero-CLS`, `Post-B-1-cure`) removed; K-6's alias fixed; M-2/F-6 re-homed — dated note at the record.

### §0.2 What this census does NOT claim (disclosed, not trimmed)

It is a census of **banked-head lines and the ids they head**, derived by reading all 66 records
whole under the rules above. It does **not** re-grade, re-scope or re-book any row: every id here
is original for life and every routing token is the record's own. Rows whose head carries **no id
token at all** (a handful of numbered roster rows headed `MISSED (R1) — heading semantics` and
the like) have no banked id to census and are **out of this file's domain by rule 1** — they are
named here as a class rather than counted, so a later seat cannot mistake their absence for an
escape. Where a record's own scoreboard states a roster count, this file's per-record row count is
**greater** (it spans every register, not the defect roster alone) — the two are different
denominators and neither corrects the other.

---

## §1 PER-RECORD TABLES

Columns: **banked id** · **aliases carried beside it** · **routing token(s), verbatim** ·
**home wave**. `unit` names the §5 file-list membership that decides every F.W3/W4 dual on the
record (rule 3's twin law); the five records with no `.a`–`.e` list are the criterion's
**negatives** and every dual on them takes the default arm to **F.W4**.

## §4 WHAT THE CENSUS FORCES (observations, not rulings — each is a derivation of §1, re-runnable against it)

1. **The F.W7 ∅ posture is TRUE AT THE CORPUS, by enumeration.** No record routes `F.W7`; the only
   two `F.W7` substrings in all 66 records are inside `KF.W7` (the keyframes relay at `fr-Tooltip`).
   `F.W6`, `F.W8` and `F.W10` are likewise named by **no record** — their wave-side populations are
   folds, operands and gate machinery, never record routings. Four passes measured F-W6/F-W8
   "escapes" against record-side rows that do not exist; §1 is the denominator that settles it.
2. **The twin law resolves 862 F.W3/W4 duals — 776 to F.W3, 86 to F.W4** *(re-summed 2026-08-29: nine duals the round-3 derivation had homed at rider waves now resolve through the criterion)* — mechanically,
   by one question of a path, with no judgement of a defect. Every one of the 86 sits on the five
   **criterion-negatives** whose target file is in no §5 unit list (`fr-BasisCanvas` ·
   `fr-ContourEditorCanvas` · `fr-EquationResult` · `fr-GalleryAdminBanner` · `fr-FourierMorphSvg`),
   which carry **289 rows** between them. §X.1-v4's stability claim — at most five records on
   the default arm, never a sixth — **holds at this census**.
3. **The alias law's magnitude is 2768 tokens** *(re-summed 2026-08-29)*. 2768 `·`/slash-joined tokens ride
   beside 4269 banked ids as aliases. A detector that atomises them into standalone identities
   mints up to 2767 bookings that no record ever made — the exact mechanism of P4-1's
   30-id manufactured tier, measured here at its full size rather than at one axis's sample.
4. **307 rows are UNROUTED** *(re-summed 2026-08-29)* — a banked head with no X-token and no terminal register anywhere
   in its own record. They are not a hole in this census; they ARE the routing frontier, and they
   concentrate in exactly two shapes: **r1-carried rosters** (`fr-ExportModal` · `fr-PaperSearch` ·
   `fr-EquationPanel` — the routings live in the superseded revision, incorporated by reference) and
   **identity-fold rows** (the route is the banked target's, in another record). Both are resolvable
   by naming, which is the one thing this programme has always been able to do.
5. **A twin divergence the census exposes and does NOT resolve.** §X.1's round-1 per-id hand-off
   table assigns some ids to **F.W4** whose file-criterion home is **F.W3** — `fr-PaperArticleWindow
   PAW-4` is the sharpest instance (`paper/PaperArticleWindow.vue` ∈ §5`.d`, so rule 3 homes it at
   F.W3, while §X.1's column 3 reads F.W4, and UNION §1c convicts F-W4 of holding zero bytes for it).
   This census applies **rule 3 as given** — the criterion, which v4's own §4 ruled admits no
   hand-picked exceptions when it moved `L-26`. The divergence is an **amendment question for the
   twin**, flagged here with its operand rather than laundered into either wave's count.

*Read-only everywhere except this file. Every id above is the record's own; every routing token is
the record's own spelling; no row is re-graded, re-scoped or re-booked here. This file is the SOLE
census operand for every gate and every future pass.*
